# Fable review queue — work that has not been through Fable's hands

**Owner instruction (2026-09-25, when Fable ran out):** "run the rest on Opus 5.5, but mark everything that has not gone
through the touch of Fable for review next time we have Fable."

**What counts as "touched by Fable".** Produced by a Fable seat, or checked by a Fable critic/judge/validator after it was
produced. Everything else is listed here and carries a marker line in the file itself:
`> PENDING FABLE REVIEW — …` (under the file title, or under the heading of the addendum/section concerned).

**Two classes.**
- **A — Fable-role work done by a stand-in.** A seat whose role is Fable's (manager, judgment, architect, validator) ran on
  Opus 5.5. These are the highest priority: the design's own quality gate depends on them.
- **B — Opus-role work no Fable seat has checked yet.** Readers, measurements, surveyors, verifiers and tool changes that
  landed after the relevant Fable critic had run (or whose Fable check was itself a stand-in).

**When Fable is available (2026-09-27: it is):** run `Workflow({ scriptPath: "/Users/cstokes/Desktop/Maps/workflows/15-fable-review-b.js", args: { "maxLanes": 4, "date": "<today>" } })`
(the extension of `05-fable-review.js` to every family in this queue; `args.families` selects area seats, `run: "sf"` reviews the
dossier-survey rows once the survey has landed). Area reviewers (per part: the fix-all rows and the amendments-b/hand-fix rows; red team;
code readings; evidence; integration; manager and corpus; sf) and one reconciler review every PENDING row, apply corrections by
blocks, replace each marker with `> Fable-reviewed <date>: <verdict>`, and set the row's status to `REVIEWED-<verdict>`.
Rows are appended at the end of the table; never rewrite the file by hand.

**Rules for anyone adding rows.** Append only. One row per artifact. Columns: id · class · what · files/sections · produced
by · date · status · what Fable should check first. Priority is implied by the check column: anything that feeds
`docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` or a delegated decision is checked first.
